**Nano Banana Pro** is Google's latest multimodal AI model featuring:

- 🎯 **Multimodal Understanding** - Process text, images, and video
- 🎨 **High-Quality Generation** - Photorealistic to artistic styles
- ⚡ **Fast Iteration** - Quick edits and variations
- 🌈 **Diverse Styles** - From pixel art to oil paintings
- 🔧 **Precise Control** - Detailed composition and lighting
- 📐 **Complex Scenes** - Multi-object, multi-character rendering

📚 **Learn More:**[Nano Banana Pro: 10 Real Cases](https://youmind.com/blog/nano-banana-pro-10-real-cases)

Some prompts support **dynamic arguments** using [Raycast Snippets](https://raycast.com/help/snippets) syntax. Look for the 🚀 Raycast Friendly badge!

**Example:**

```
A quote card with "{argument name="quote" default="Stay hungry, stay foolish"}"
by {argument name="author" default="Steve Jobs"}
```

When used in Raycast, you can dynamically replace the arguments for quick iterations!

> ⭐ Hand-picked by our team for exceptional quality and creativity

#### 📖 Description

A prompt for generating a wide quote card featuring a famous person’s portrait, with a brown background, light-gold serif quote text, and layout where text occupies two-thirds and the person one-third. The quote text and author are parameterized for reuse.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A wide quote card featuring a famous person, with a brown background and a light-gold serif font for the quote: “{argument name="famous_quote" default="Stay Hungry, Stay Foolish"}” and smaller text: “—{argument name="author" default="Steve Jobs"}.” There is a large, subtle quotation mark before the text. The portrait of the person is on the left, the text on the right. The text occupies two-thirds of the image and the portrait one-third, with a slight gradient transition effect on the portrait.
```

#### 📖 Description

Create an Infographics with bento grid 8 module layout, user can specify any product name in Food, Medicine, tech etc category, choose language, Background style, Hero grid style

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Input Variable: [insert product name]
Language: [insert language]

System Instruction:
Create an image of premium liquid glass Bento grid product infographic with 8 modules (card 2 to 8 show text titles only).
1) Product Analysis:
→ Identify product's dominant natural color → "hero color"
→ Identify category: FOOD / MEDICINE / TECH
2) Color Palette (derived from hero):
→ Product + accents: full saturation hero color
→ Icons, borders: muted hero (30-40% saturation, never black)
3) Visual Style:
→ Hero product: real photography (authentic, premium), 3D Glass version [choose one]
→ Cards: Apple liquid glass (85-90% transparent) with Whisper-thin borders and Subtle drop shadow for floating depth and reflecting the background color
→ Background stays behind cards and high blur where cards are [choose one]:
- Ethereal: product essence, light caustics, abstract glow
- Macro: product texture close-up, heavily blurred
- Pattern: product repeated softly at 10-15% opacity
- Context: relevant environment, blurred + desaturated
→ Add subtle motion effect
→ Asymmetric Bento grid, 16:9 landscape
→ Hero card: 28-30% | Info modules: 70-72%
4) Module Content (8 Cards):
M1 — Hero: Product displayed as real photo / 3D glass / stylized interpretation (choose one)in beautiful form + product name label
M2 — Core Benefits: 4 unique benefits + hero-color icons
M3 — How to Use: 4 usage methods + icons
M4 — Key Metrics: 5 EXACT data points
Format: [icon] [Label] [Bold Value] [Unit]
FOOD: Calories: [X] kcal/100g, Carbs: [X]g (fiber [X]g, sugar [X]g), Protein: [X]g, [Key Vitamin]: [X]mg ([X]% DV), [Key Mineral]: [X]mg ([X]% DV)
MEDICINE:Active: [name], Strength: [X] mg, Onset: [X] min, Duration: [X] hrs, Half-life: [X] hrs
TECH:Chip: [model], Battery: [X] hrs, Weight: [X]g,[Key spec]: [value], Connectivity: [protocols]
M5 — Who It's For: 4 recommended groups with green checkmark icons | 3 caution groups with amber warning icons
M6 — Important Notes: 4 precautions + warning icons
M7 — Quick Reference:
→ FOOD: Glycemic Index + dietary tags with icons
→ MEDICINE: Side effects + severity with icons
→ TECH: Compatibility + certifications with icons
M8 — Did You Know: 3 facts (origin, science, global stat) + icons
Output: 1 image, 16:9 landscape, ultra-premium liquid glass infographic.
```

#### 📖 Description

Prompt for a hand-drawn style header image of a person introducing Nano Banana Pro

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Completely recreate the uploaded person.
Make it a header image for a note article where that person introduces “Nano Banana Pro”.
Aspect ratio: horizontal 16:9.
Style and colors: simple, hand-drawn style, italic, with a blue and green gradient.
Title text: “In-depth explanation of Google’s new AI ‘Nano Banana Pro’”.
```

#### 📖 Description

A detailed multi-panel prompt for Nano Banana Pro, creating a 2x2 grid photo collage where a single female character, in four different outfits and settings, pieces together a puzzle that spells '2026 New Year's Day Happy' in the center. The prompt specifies precise facial feature retention, clothing details, background elements, and photographic parameters for a fashion magazine style.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
[Key: Maintain precise facial features, retain original face structure, the character in the image must be completely consistent with the uploaded reference image] High-end photo studio 2x2 grid photo. Top-left panel (Navy Blue background): The character wears a navy blue uniform-style dress, decorated with gold buttons, vintage curls with a blue beret and pearl earrings. She holds up a huge puzzle piece (top-left piece, with the number "20" on it) with both hands, moving it towards the center of the frame. Her eyes are focused on the central puzzle area, her expression is serious, with a slight smile. The background features navy stripes, an anchor, and the text "Set Sail for the New Year". Top-right panel (Cherry Blossom Pink background): The same woman wears a pink lace dress, a pearl necklace, a princess hairstyle with a pink rose hairpin and crystal earrings. She holds up the top-right puzzle piece (with the number "26" on it) with both hands, moving it towards the center to connect with the top-left piece. Her eyes look at the puzzle seam, her expression is focused and expectant, and her body leans forward. The background features pink cherry blossoms, the text "Beautiful Encounter", butterflies, and petals. Bottom-left panel (Mint Green background): The same woman wears a mint green cotton and linen dress, in an artistic style, with natural long hair, a green hairband, and wooden earrings. She holds up the bottom-left puzzle piece (with the text "New Year's Day" on it) with both hands, moving it upwards to connect with the top-left piece. Her eyes look at the puzzle, her expression is serious, and her mouth is slightly pursed. The background features green plants, the text "Hope Grows", new sprouts, and leaves. Bottom-right panel (Lemon Yellow background): The same woman wears a yellow dress with a sunflower pattern, pigtails with yellow bows. She pushes in the last bottom-right puzzle piece (with the text "Happy" on it) to complete the puzzle. The four pieces perfectly form the complete pattern "2026 New Year's Day Happy" in the center of the frame. She tilts her head back, looking at the completed puzzle, her face beaming with a successful, joyful smile. The center of the frame bursts with golden light and confetti. The background features a yellow sun, the text "Complete Success", smiley faces, and sunflowers. The puzzle pieces converge from the four corners to the center to form a complete picture. Clear makeup, bright ring light, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, four-panel composition with puzzle interaction, fashion magazine style.
```

#### 📖 Description

A prompt designed to generate an image of a vintage United States patent document from the late 1800s. It specifies details like precise technical drawings, handwritten annotations, aged paper texture, foxing stains, an embossed seal, and a wax stamp, making it ideal for historical or archival aesthetics.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A vintage patent document for {argument name="invention" default="INVENTION"}, styled after late 1800s United States Patent Office filings. The page features precise technical drawings with numbered callouts (Fig. 1, Fig. 2, Fig. 3) showing front, side, and exploded views. Handwritten annotations in fountain-pen ink describe mechanisms. The paper is aged ivory with foxing stains and soft fold creases. An official embossed seal and red wax stamp appear in the corner. A hand-signed inventor's name and date appear at the bottom. The entire image feels like a recovered archival document—authoritative, historic, and slightly mysterious.
```

#### 📖 Description

A very detailed Nano Banana prompt describing a female mirror selfie in a blue-toned otaku computer corner, with full specifications for character, environment, lighting, camera, and negative prompts.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
### Scene
Mirror selfie in an otaku-style computer corner, blue color tone.

### Subject
* Gender expression: female
* Age: around 25
* Ethnicity: East Asian
* Body type: slim, with a defined waist; natural body proportions
* Skin tone: light neutral tone
* Hairstyle:
* Length: waist-length hair
* Style: straight with slightly curled ends
* Color: medium brown
* Pose:
* Stance: standing in a slight contrapposto pose
* Right hand: holding a smartphone in front of her face (identity hidden)
* Left arm: naturally hanging down alongside the torso
* Torso: body leaning slightly back; waist and abdomen exposed
* Clothing:
* Top: light blue cropped knit cardigan, top two buttons fastened; a blue French-style bra faintly visible
* Bottom: denim ultra-short shorts, with a blue satin ribbon bow on each side of the hips
* Socks: blue and white horizontal striped over-the-knee socks
* Accessory: a blue cute mascot phone case

### Environment
* Description: bedroom computer corner seen through a wall-mounted mirror
* Furnishings:
* White desk
* Single monitor showing a soft blue wallpaper (no readable text)
* Mechanical keyboard with white keycaps on a blue desk mat
* Mouse on a small blue mouse pad
* PC tower on the right side with blue case lighting
* Three anime figures on or near the PC tower
* A poster of a pagoda on the wall
* Cat-shaped desk lamp with blue accents
* A transparent glass of water
* A tall green leafy plant by the window (on the left side of the frame)
* Color replacement: replace all originally pink elements (clothes and room decor) with blue tones (baby blue to sky blue/periwinkle blue).

### Lighting
* Light source: daylight coming from a large window on the left side of the camera, through sheer curtains
* Light quality: soft, diffused light
* White balance (K): 5200

### Camera
* Mode: smartphone rear camera shooting via the mirror (no portrait/bokeh mode)
* Equivalent focal length (mm): 26
* Distances (m):
* Subject to mirror: 0.6
* Camera to mirror: 0.5
* Exposure:
* Aperture (f): 1.8
* ISO: 100
* Shutter speed (s): 0.01
* Exposure compensation (EV): -0.3
* Focus: focus on the torso and shorts in the mirror image
* Depth of field: natural smartphone deep depth of field; background clearly visible with no artificial blur
* Composition:
* Aspect ratio: 1:1
* Crop: from the top of the head to mid-thigh; include the desk, monitor, PC tower, and plant in the frame
* Angle: slightly high angle from the mirror’s point of view
* Composition note: keep the subject centered; to avoid wide-angle edge distortion, have her stand a bit further away and crop to a square later.

### Negative prompts
* Any appearance of pink/magenta anywhere
* Beauty filters/over-smoothed skin; poreless skin look
* Exaggerated or distorted anatomy
* NSFW, see-through fabrics, wardrobe malfunctions
* Logos, brand names, or readable user interface text
* Fake portrait-mode blur, CGI/illustration feel
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly-structured image prompt to depict a modern-day scene reimagined as an Edo-period Japanese Ukiyo-e woodblock print, with detailed guidance on anachronistic tech, composition, texture, and color.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A Japanese Edo-period Ukiyo-e woodblock print. The overall feeling is a surreal collaboration between masters like Hokusai and Hiroshige, reimagining modern technology through an ancient lens.

**The scene:** {argument name="modern scene" default="a busy Shibuya scramble crossing"}

**Edo transformation logic:**
Characters wear Edo-era kimono but perform modern actions. All technology is transformed into surreal Edo equivalents:
* Smartphones are glowing, illustrated paper scrolls being read intently.
* Metro stations and trains are giant articulated wooden centipede carriages shuffling through crowds.
* Skyscrapers are reimagined as endless, towering wooden pagodas reaching into dramatic clouds.
* Robots and mecha appear as giant, armored woodblock golems.

The composition uses a flattened perspective with large, bold, hand-carved ink outlines. The background features heavily stylized Ukiyo-e wave patterns and dramatic, swirling clouds, with a distant Mt. Fuji visible on the horizon.

The image must look like a physical print, not a digital painting.
* Texture: strong visible wood grain texture and rough paper fibers throughout the piece.
* Printing imperfections: pigment bleeding is evident. Simulate hand-pressed plates with slight color misalignment for authenticity.
* Color palette: strictly limited to traditional mineral pigments, with dominant use of Prussian blue, vermilion red, and muted yellow ochre.
* Lighting: soft, flat, shadow-free lighting with no digital gradients.

Aspect ratio is 3:4 vertical poster. Include vertical Japanese calligraphy describing the scene and a traditional red artist seal stamp in a corner.
```

She is playfully sticking her tongue out toward the camera, giving a flirty, casual expression.\n\nShe is wearing a fitted dark grey/black top and a delicate necklace with a small cross pendant. One arm is partially visible extended upward holding the phone, slightly entering the top frame and creating a candid, unfiltered feel.\n\nThe environment is a soft, cozy indoor setting, likely a bedroom with neutral tones (white/cream bedding and pillow). Lighting is natural and diffused, likely from a nearby window, creating even illumination across her face with soft highlights and minimal shadows.\n\nThe image has a relaxed, intimate, social-media style aesthetic (Instagram/Snapchat vibe), with realistic skin texture, slight lens softness, and natural color grading.",

#### 📖 Description

A highly detailed prompt for generating a close-up selfie of a young woman in the Korean Ulzzang style, emphasizing specific makeup details (glass skin, aegyo sal, cherry pink glossy lips), outfit, pose (biting index finger), and lighting (direct flash in a dark indoor setting). Requires a face reference.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create A Close-up selfie of a beautiful young woman.
Skin: Porcelain white, bright, smooth, and poreless.
Face: Symmetrical; do not change the original face.
Style: Edgy and mysterious.
Pose: Standing in a room (likely a living room). Head tilted down, looking towards the camera. Calm expression with lips slightly parted, biting the index finger.
Camera Angle: Low angle selfie.
Outfit & Accessories:
Outfit:  reference photo 2.
Accessories: Small thin necklace with a rose pendant; medium-sized hoop earrings.
Nails: Light brown with white tips (French tip style).
Makeup (Detailed Korean Ulzzang Style):
Skin Finish: Super smooth glass skin, milky bright tone, thin dewy glowing foundation.
Highlighting: Soft highlights on the forehead, nose, and chin.
Eyebrows: Straight,tebal  natural, soft,.
Eye Makeup: Soft pink-peach gradient with subtle shimmer; very thin "puppy" eyeliner; long, curly, naturally separated eyelashes.
Under-eye: Clear and glowing "aegyo sal" with small highlights; brightened under-eye area.
Blush: Baby pink "over-blush" spreading across the cheeks and bridge of the nose.
Nose & Lips: Small slim nose with soft contour; "cherry pink" glossy jelly lips with a gradient (darker on the inside); well-defined cupid's bow.
Expression: Innocent, cute, and youthful.
Hair:
Length & Color: Very long, dark brown hair.
Texture: Smooth and shiny.
Style: Straight on top, wavy/curly at the bottom.
Bangs: curtain bangs,dan helaian rambut menutupi wajahnya,slightly messy on top.
Bagian atasnya sedikit berantakan
Environment & Quality:
Background: Dark indoor setting, showing a blurred door and sofa.
Atmosphere: Nighttime.
Lighting: Direct flash focusing on the subject, especially the face, creating a bright and glowing effect.
Technical Quality: HD, 8k high resolution.
Aspect Ratio: 9:16.
Don't change face
```

```
]
```

#### 📖 Description

A prompt for generating a hyper-stylized, cinematic portrait of a person, emphasizing dramatic dual-tone lighting. One side of the face is illuminated by cool blue light, and the other by vivid pink/red neon, creating a high-contrast, bold look while maintaining the subject's exact identity.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A hyper-stylized portrait of the same person, keeping their exact face and identity unchanged. They are captured in a thoughtful pose with one hand resting on their chin, illuminated by dramatic dual-tone lighting. One side of the face is lit with cool blue light, while the other side glows with vivid pink/red neon tones, creating a bold, high-contrast look.
They are wearing a dark top and a watch. The background remains dark and minimal, emphasizing the colorful illumination on the face and upper body. Cinematic, sharp, and highly detailed.
```

#### 📖 Description

Two separate prompts for generating ultra-realistic portraits of the same woman, focusing on different lighting and composition styles. Prompt 1 uses soft natural window light for a cinematic, side-angle portrait, while Prompt 2 uses even soft studio lighting for a clean, front-facing professional headshot.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Prompt 1 (First Image – Blue Top, Side Angle Portrait):

Ultra-realistic close-up portrait of a beautiful woman with light green eyes and soft freckles, auburn curly hair styled in a loose messy bun with natural wispy bangs framing her face. She is wearing a deep blue V-neck top. Shot at a slight side angle with her head turned toward the camera, confident and calm expression. Soft natural window light casting gentle shadows on one side of her face, neutral beige background. Minimal makeup, natural skin texture visible, cinematic lighting, shallow depth of field, 85mm lens, high resolution, professional fashion photography, sharp focus on eyes, soft bokeh background.

⸻

Prompt 2 (Second Image – Front-Facing Hoodie Portrait):

Hyper-realistic front-facing portrait of a woman with light green eyes, subtle freckles across her cheeks and nose, auburn hair parted in the center and tied back in a low loose bun with soft strands framing her face. She is wearing a dark grey hoodie. Neutral expression, direct eye contact with camera. Even soft studio lighting, minimal shadows, clean white background. Natural skin texture, no heavy makeup, high detail, 85mm lens, shallow depth of field, professional headshot photography, ultra sharp focus, realistic skin tones, 4K resolution.
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly detailed, structured prompt for generating a hyper-realistic mirror selfie of an attractive woman (Ana de Armas, Iggy Azalea, or Sophie Rain likeness) in a seductive business-casual pose. The prompt emphasizes specific clothing (pink bodysuit, glossy pantyhose, extremely tall yellow patent leather thigh-high boots), lighting (purple LED ambiance), and detailed reflections.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"image_type": "photorealistic bedroom mirror selfie with laptop, 8K ultra-detailed, sharp focus, masterpiece",
"subject": {
"role": "seductive business-casual pose woman with laptop",
"gender": "female",
"age": "20-24",
"appearance": "attractive young woman, tanned/bronze skin with glossy/oiled sheen on legs, arms and face, long dark blonde hair in high sleek ponytail with loose framing strands, sharp winged eyeliner, long lashes, filled brows, glossy nude-pink lips, playful confident smirk looking at camera via mirror",
"expression": "slight teasing smile, eyes locked on reflection, head turned over shoulder, confident and inviting vibe",
"clothing": "tight long-sleeve pink bodysuit / minidress with deep V-neckline and ruched front detailing (stretchy fabric, form-fitting), black sheer glossy pantyhose / tights with strong shine",
"accessories": "extremely tall yellow patent leather platform thigh-high boots (lace-up style, thick platform sole, very high stiletto heel, glossy finish), thin bracelet on wrist",
"pose": "sitting sideways on light gray upholstered chair, legs crossed at thighs, torso twisted toward mirror, right hand holding pink iPhone for selfie at chest height, left hand resting on thigh, laptop (Apple MacBook) open on lap, strong arched back, hips lifted, emphasis on curves, legs and boots",
"body_details": "athletic-curvy figure, pronounced glutes and thighs, toned legs, realistic skin texture with oil sheen, fabric stretch on dress, glossy pantyhose reflections, detailed boot leather and platform shine"
},
"environment": {
"location": "modern bright bedroom with purple LED ambiance",
"backdrop": "light gray walls, white curtains or closet doors, large green indoor plant (Monstera or similar) visible behind, purple LED strip lights along ceiling and walls creating soft violet/purple glow, white shaggy rug on wooden floor, string lights or fairy lights partially visible, yellow neon written "KeorUnreal' on wall, wardrobe/closet in background, clean luxurious yet cozy interior, a futuristic painting on wall",
"lighting": "bright natural window light + dominant purple LED strip glow + soft warm fill from room lamps, glossy highlights on skin, pantyhose, boots and dress, dramatic rim lighting on curves, purple color cast on entire scene"
},
"camera": {
"angle": "mirror selfie perspective (slight low-to-medium angle)",
"lens": "wide-angle phone lens (natural slight edge distortion), shallow depth of field, razor-sharp focus on face, ponytail, dress, pantyhose, boots, laptop and tire stack",
"composition": "subject dominant in frame, mirror reflection showing clear side/rear pose, purple LED glow filling background, laptop centered on lap, phone visible in hand"
},
"style": "hyper-realistic influencer bedroom mirror selfie, ultra-detailed text"
}
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly technical prompt using ControlNet parameters (OpenPose and Depth) to generate a realistic mirror selfie of a young woman (Madison Beer likeness) in a deep forest green strawberry-patterned onesie. The prompt strictly controls pose, depth, and anatomical proportions to ensure realism and avoid distortion, set in a bathroom environment.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"subject": "young woman with long natural black hair and fair skin, wearing a form-fitting, long-sleeved, short-legged deep forest green onesie patterned with small strawberries.",
"pose": "Standing in profile, facing left, torso slightly turned to glance back at the mirror. Right hand holds a black smartphone to take a selfie. Natural standing posture with relaxed shoulders and a soft, subtle smile.",
"environment": "Bathroom setting. A white paneled door with silver hardware is directly behind. A white sink basin and chrome faucet occupy the bottom foreground. A white light switch is visible on the left wall.",
"camera": "Mirror selfie perspective. Shot from chest height, cropping the subject from mid-thigh to just above the head.",
"lighting": "Standard indoor artificial overhead lighting, casting soft shadows on the door panels and bright reflections on the metallic faucet.",
"mood_and_expression": "Casual, relaxed selfie posture with a calm and natural expression.",
"style_and_realism": "Unedited, realistic smartphone photograph.",
"colors_and_tone": "Warm overall tone dominated by whites, fair skin hues, deep green fabric tones, soft red strawberry accents, and metallic silver/chrome.",
"quality_and_technical_details": "Typical modern smartphone camera resolution with slight indoor grain and realistic fabric texture.",
"aspect_ratio_and_output": "3:4",
"controlnet": {
"pose_control": {
"model_type": "openpose_full",
"purpose": "Preserve exact skeletal alignment, torso orientation, and anatomical proportions without altering mass distribution",
"constraints": "No limb length change, no torso compression, no posture exaggeration, no anatomical distortion",
"recommended_weight": 1.1
},
"depth_control": {
"model_type": "depth_midas",
"purpose": "Preserve accurate 3D spatial depth, volumetric projection, and foreground-background separation",
"constraints": "No depth flattening, no volumetric distortion, no perspective alteration",
"recommended_weight": 1.15
}
},
"negative_prompt": "stylization, body reshaping, anatomical distortion, exaggerated curves, volume compression, proportion changes, depth flattening, beautification filters, symmetry correction, perspective alteration, explicit content"
}
```

#### 📖 Description

A prompt for generating a photorealistic image of an attractive woman taking a mirror selfie in a modern gym. The prompt specifies details about her outfit, pose (back slightly turned, looking over shoulder), expression, hair, and accessories like painted nails and workout gloves.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create an image of an attractive woman wearing a light blue athletic gym outfit, including a fitted sports top and matching shorts with a bold minimalist logo text on the front. She is taking a mirror selfie inside a modern gym environment. Her expression is calm and serene. Her hair is styled in a medium-sized low bun at the back of her head. She has medium-length dark blue painted nails and is wearing white workout gloves. She is standing with her back slightly turned, one leg placed forward in a confident pose, looking over her shoulder toward the mirror. She holds a mint green modern smartphone in her right hand.
```

Left arm is not visible., n/a, wearing thin black-framed glasses. Setting: A cozy and warm indoor setting, likely a living room. The subject is resting on a light gray or beige couch with a visible fabric texture. The background is softly blurred, suggesting a comfortable home environment.. Illuminated by A large, soft, diffused light source, likely a window with natural light, positioned to the front-left of the subject.. Shadows: features soft, diffused shadows with gentle, feathered edges. The primary shadows are cast to the right side of the subject and behind her, indicating a light source from the front-left. A highly detailed, photorealistic prompt for Nano Banana 2 via Hailuo AI to generate an image resembling Anya Taylor-Joy on a yacht deck at twilight. The prompt specifies physique (curvy hourglass, heavy bust), outfit (tight white ruched mini-dress), pose (seated, index finger touching lip), lighting (soft diffused twilight), and style (unedited raw aesthetic, high anatomical fidelity).

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"subject": {
"desc": "{argument name="subject resemblance" default="Anya Taylor-Joy"} resemblance, fair skin with freckles/pores/natural texture, long messy windswept shoulder-length blonde hair",
"physique": "Fit athletic curvy hourglass, prominent heavy bust with strong forward/outward projection and deep cleavage, natural soft tissue behavior",
"outfit": "Tight short white ruched mini-dress with thin criss-cross halter straps",
"accessories": "Silver cross necklace on thin chain, right hand holding partially filled white wine glass"
},
"pose": {
"position": "Seated comfortably on white cushions, torso angled forward with shoulders rolled inward to emphasize chest depth/cleavage, weight slightly back",
"arms": "Right arm low beside hip holding wine glass by stem, left arm raised with index finger lightly touching lower lip/teeth",
"head": "Slightly tilted downward, eyes looking up directly at camera",
"legs": "Close together, upper thighs visible",
"expression": "Playful alluring relaxed, seductive slight subtle smile, intense direct eye contact"
},
"environment": {
"location": "Outdoor deck of yacht/boat at twilight",
"foreground": "Plush white upholstered cushions",
"background": "Polished stainless steel boat railings, calm dark blue ocean to horizon, soft muted blue twilight sky",
"props": "Modern smartphone face-down on cushion beside right hip"
},
"camera": {
"shot": "Mid-shot from mid-thighs up to slightly above head, vertical 3:4",
"angle": "Eye-level or slightly elevated looking down at face",
"dof": "Medium, sharp focus on subject, slightly blurred background ocean/railings",
"perspective": "Strong 3D depth capturing natural body volume"
},
"lighting": "Soft diffused evening ambient light, frontal soft illumination (possibly boat lights + twilight), gentle highlights on face/chest/white fabric, accurate subsurface scattering on skin, no harsh shadows",
"mood": "Playful alluring relaxed, seductive with intense direct eye contact, twilight luxury yacht atmosphere",
"style": "Photorealistic candid lifestyle photography, unedited raw aesthetic, high anatomical fidelity, no beauty filters/airbrushing, natural skin imperfections/fabric folds",
"colors": "Cool oceanic blues/muted grey-blue sky background, crisp bright white dress/cushions contrast, warm natural skin tones, golden amber wine in glass",
"quality": "High fidelity crisp resolution, intricate details: individual messy hair strands, freckles/pores, ruched fabric micro-texture, realistic light refraction through wine glass, 8K",
"aspect_ratio": "3:4",
"negative_prompt": "small/reduced/flattened bust, lifted breasts, slimmed torso, proportion averaging, plastic/airbrushed skin, beautification, stylization, depth flattening, wide distortion, mirror selfies, phone-in-hand visib"
```

```
"subject": {
```

#### 📖 Description

A prompt template for creating a high-resolution graphic design collage for a birthday, featuring a three-panel photo strip of a young woman (requiring a reference image) integrated with jagged red and yellow torn-paper graphics and custom text.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A high-resolution graphic design collage featuring a tilted, three-panel white photo strip of smiling young woman  [Use the uploaded image as reference], she wears a red sweater and thick beige knit scarf, shot with bright studio lighting. The strip features a young woman with dark curly hair in three different playful poses. The photo strip overlaps jagged red and yellow torn-paper graphics against a slate-gray background that features faint, oversized monochromatic portrait of the woman. Vertical white text reading "HAPPY BIRTHDAY {argument name="name" default="[NAME]"}" with dotted red and yellow lines borders the left edge. The mood is joyful and celebratory, seamlessly blending photorealistic photography with sharp vector graphic elements. Vertical portrait, 4k.
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly structured prompt for Nano Banana 2 via Hailuo AI to generate a photorealistic image of a female subject (cosplaying as a character from Overwatch) taking a selfie in a detailed gaming room environment. The prompt specifies body details, pose (winking, tongue out, leg raised), attire, accessories (gaming headset, smartphone), and extensive environmental elements (monitors, decor, furniture).

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"subject": {
"gender": "Female",
"cosplay": "blonde as {argument name="cosplay character" default="https://t.co/kAhoun6OXc from Overwatch"}",
"hair": {
"color": "Blonde",
"style": "Pigtails with purple accessories"
},
"expression": {
"face": "Winking, tongue sticking out slightly"
},
"pose": "Sitting in a gaming chair, taking a selfie with a Samsung Galaxy S25 Ultra smartphone in the right hand, left leg raised high showing the sole of the pink puma shoe, right leg bent at the knee",
"accessories": {
"headset": "white gaming headset around neck",
"smartphone": "orange smartphone in right hand"
},
"tattoos": "A snake and japan symbol Visible on left arm and right leg"
},
"attire": {
"bodysuit": {
"color": "orange and white",
"text": "WANNA PLAY?"
},
"socks": {
"style": "Thigh-high, gold and silver striped"
},
"shoes": {
"style": "Shoes from attached picture"
}
},
"environment": {
"location": "Gaming room",
"furniture": {
"chair": "light blue and red Autofull gaming chair",
"desk": "White gaming desk",
"drawers": "White IKEA Alex drawer unit"
},
"equipment": {
"monitors": "Two screens, one vertical, one horizontal showing desktop",
"keyboard": "Partially visible",
"mouse": "Partially visible"
},
"decor": {
"sign": "Light-up sign reading 'KEORUNREAL GAMES'",
"plush_toys": "Multiple, including different labubu",
"posters": "Framed manga dragonball and onepiece artwork on the wall",
"rug": "Fluffy yellow rug"
}
},
"lighting": "Soft, indoor lighting",
"camera_angle": "slightly high angle",
"image_type": "Photograph, high-quality, cinematic, Ultra-realistic photo and lighting, high quality photo, depth of field, no Ai or cgi look."
}
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly structured JSON prompt for generating a 3x3 fashion editorial grid featuring a single adult woman with consistent identity across all 9 frames. The styling focuses on a red satin blouse, denim jeans, and translucent red heart-shaped sunglasses, specifying modern fashion lighting and various poses.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"generation_request": {
"meta_data": {
"tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
"task_type": "editorial_pop_campaign_grid",
"language": "en",
"priority": "highest",
"version": "v2.0_RED_SATIN_HEART_GLASSES_GRID"
},

"input": {
"mode": "text_to_image",
"notes": "Create a 3x3 fashion editorial grid featuring one adult woman with consistent identity across all 9 frames. High-fashion studio campaign style."
},

"output_settings": {
"aspect_ratio": "1:1",
"resolution_target": "ultra_high_res",
"layout": {
"type": "grid",
"rows": 3,
"cols": 3,
"gutter": "thin",
"outer_border": "none"
},
"render_style": "high_fashion_studio",
"sharpness": "editorial_crisp",
"grain": "none"
},

"scene": {
"environment": "minimal studio with soft warm beige or light blush backdrop",
"lighting": {
"style": "modern fashion campaign lighting",
"key_light": "large softbox front",
"fill": "balanced soft fill",
"shadow": "clean sculpted fashion shadow"
},
"camera": {
"lens": "85mm and slight wide variation for dynamic angles",
"focus": "sharp on eyes and fabric texture"
}
},

"styling": {
"top": "luxury red satin blouse, soft sheen, slightly draped",
"bottom": "classic mid-rise blue denim jeans, structured fit",
"nails": "glossy deep red manicure",
"glasses": "translucent red heart-shaped sunglasses",
"makeup": "clean skin, soft contour, red-toned lips",
"hair": "sleek straight bob or soft blowout"
},

"poses": [
"close-up smiling holding sunglasses",
"low-angle confident gaze",
"over-shoulder fashion look",
"hands framing face",
"playful wink",
"dynamic lean forward",
"sitting casual chic pose",
"intense direct eye contact",
"subtle smirk luxury expression"
],

"mood_keywords": [
"playful high fashion",
"pop editorial",
"modern campaign",
"red monochrome accent",
"cool girl energy"
],

"quality_control": {
"identity_lock": "strict",
"fabric_physics": "realistic satin behavior",
"avoid": [
"cheap Instagram filter look",
"oversaturated red",
"plastic texture",
"misaligned sunglasses"
]
}
}
}
```

```
},
"scene": {
"festival": "Holi",
"location_type": "traditional_indian_town_street",
"time_of_day": "late_morning",
"lighting": {
"source": "natural_sunlight",
"quality": "bright_warm_golden",
"atmosphere": "colorful_powder_mist_in_air",
"dynamic_range": "high",
"sun_rays_visible_through_powder": true
},
"environment_details": {
"architecture": "traditional_indian_town_houses_with_balconies",

"small_local_shops",
"colorful_buntings_strung_across_street",
"hanging_marigold_garlands",
"festival_banners",
"scattered_gulal_powder_on_ground"
],
"background_activity": [

"colorful_smoke_clouds_rising"
]
}
},
"main_subject": {
"gender": "female",
"ethnicity": "Indian",
"age_range": "early_20s",
"appearance": {
"face": {

"bright_pink_smears_on_cheeks",
"yellow_powder_on_forehead",
"soft_color_dust_on_nose_and_chin"
]
},
"hair": {
```

```
"Intense highlight on the noodle cup and face"
]
},

}
}
```

#### 📖 Description

A prompt for Google Gemini Nano Banana 2 to generate an ultra-wide fisheye lens perspective shot from ground level, featuring a young woman crouching mid-air between brick buildings, creating strong foreground distortion and depth. The prompt requires using an image for face reference.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Make an Ultra-wide fisheye lens perspective shot from ground level looking straight up at a young woman use image for face reference crouching mid-air between brick buildings forming a circular frame around the sky. She reaches one hand dramatically toward the camera, fingers spread wide, creating strong foreground distortion and depth. Dynamic action pose, confident expression, casual streetwear outfit: fitted white t-shirt, blue straight-leg jeans, red and white sneakers. Urban European neighborhood setting with brick houses, tiled roofs, windows visible around the edges. Bright daylight, clear blue sky, natural lighting, sharp focus, high detail, cinematic composition, exaggerated perspective, 8k resolution, photorealistic, adventure photography style.
```

#### 📖 Description

A complex, multi-step prompt designed for Nano Banana 2 to invent an absurd, legally patentable gadget by combining three random home appliances. The process involves generating a formal patent document (title, abstract, claims, problem solved), technical patent drawings, and a final glossy product render in Apple-product-photography style.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Combine these three objects into one single absurd but legally patentable invention. Output:
Official patent title (overly long)
USPTO-style abstract (150 words)
4 ridiculous ‘claims’
One real-sounding problem it solves Style: 100% serious US patent office document.

Create four clean black-and-white technical patent drawings (exploded view, side, top, perspective) of this new invention in classic patent illustration style.

Now render the final product in glossy Apple-product-photography style on white infinity background with subtle reflection. Add fake patent number in corner.
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly structured, variable-driven prompt template for generating an ornate box diorama based on Jules Verne's 'Twenty Thousand Leagues Under the Sea'. The prompt uses variables for theme, materials, style, and scene descriptions across three shelves, along with strict directives for framing, composition, and lighting to ensure a miniature scale aesthetic.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Variable Guide
1. OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME = Scenes from the book  Twenty Thousand leagues under the sea  by Jules Verne, in the order in which they are presented in the book
2. CUPBOARD_BASE_MATERIAL =
3. CUPBOARD_LINING_MATERIAL =
4. RACK_MATERIAL =
5. ORNAMENTATION_STYLE = [e.g., Gothic carved wood, Art Deco brass, Elven interlaced vines]
6. ART_STYLE = Hyper-Realistic Render
7. TOP_SHELF_SCENE = [Describe specific actions of miniature figures defining the scene]
8. MIDDLE_SHELF_SCENE = [Describe specific actions of miniature figures defining the scene]
9. BOTTOM_SHELF_SCENE = [Describe specific actions of miniature figures defining the scene]
10. LIGHTING =
11. CAMERA_ANGLE = A straight-on, front-facing view, captured from a moderately high angle looking distinctly downward.
12. ASPECT_RATIO = 3:4

Generation Directives
(Note: If any variable is left blank, intelligently infer the most appropriate detail based on the OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME)

Directive 1: Framing & Composition (Highest Priority)
A direct, front-facing photograph captured from CAMERA_ANGLE. The composition must perfectly center the open display structure, looking down at a distinct angle into the shelves to clearly reveal the floor plates and top-down depth of the miniature objects within the dioramas, while ensuring the exterior side panels remain completely hidden. The entire free-standing structure must be completely visible with negative space around it against a simple background. The aspect ratio is 3:4.

Directive 2: The Main Structure (Strict Constraints)
Render the image in the style of ART_STYLE. The central object is a highly detailed, permanently open-front curio display structure. It must not possess doors, hinges, or door frames of any kind. The exterior is constructed from CUPBOARD_BASE_MATERIAL and detailed with ORNAMENTATION_STYLE. The interior back wall is lined with CUPBOARD_LINING_MATERIAL, and the individual shelves are made of RACK_MATERIAL.

Directive 3: The Miniature Dioramas & Figures
The structure houses a cohesive miniature world divided across three shelves, adhering to the OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME. Crucially, each shelf must contain miniature figures engaged in a specific narrative action.
* Top Shelf: Miniature figures are depicted [TOP_SHELF_SCENE].
* Middle Shelf: Miniature figures are depicted [MIDDLE_SHELF_SCENE].
* Bottom Shelf: Miniature figures are depicted [BOTTOM_SHELF_SCENE].
Ensure the scale clearly reads as miniature dioramas housed within a furniture piece.

Directive 4: Atmosphere & Lighting
The scene is illuminated by LIGHTING. The lighting should cast appropriate micro-shadows within each shelf to emphasize the depth, the figures, and the miniature scale of the dioramas.
```

#### 📖 Description

A multi-step prompt designed to act as a literary recommendation engine and antique typewriter automaton builder. It instructs the AI to analyze a specified book (1984) and select four obscure, intellectually resonant books, then visualize them in a 2x2 grid featuring vintage typewriters and 3D paper sculptures of symbolic scenes.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
<instructions>for {argument name="book title" default="1984"}
Act as a literary deep-dive recommendation engine + antique typewriter automaton builder.
Step 1: Analyze the input book—narrative voice, philosophical undercurrents, world atmosphere, and character psychology.
Step 2: Select 4 obscure books with mirroring intellectual/emotional resonance.
Step 3: ONE 2x2 grid. Each panel features a vintage typewriter with exposed mechanics; keys strike to "type" floating 3D paper sculptures forming key symbolic scenes; ink ribbons spell year subtly. Under each: typed label “Author – Title (Year)”.
Style: dimly lit writer’s study, warm desk lamp, mechanical patina, floating paper fragments.
</instructions>
```

#### 📖 Description

A prompt template for generating a high-resolution, professional technical infographic of an object. It requires a clean, semi-realistic cutaway view showing internal components, automatic labeling with technical terminology, and a balanced layout suitable for an engineering manual.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a high-resolution professional infographic of [{argument name="object name" default="OBJECT NAME"}].
Display the object in a clean, semi-realistic technical illustration style. Include both external structure and internal components using a cutaway view, cross-section, or exploded layout where appropriate.
Automatically detect and label all major structural, mechanical, electronic, or functional components using accurate technical terminology.
Use thin leader lines connecting each label precisely to the correct part. Ensure labels are clear, readable, and professionally formatted.
Add a bold headline at the top:
“OBJECT NAME – Complete Structural Breakdown”
Layout should be balanced and organized like a modern engineering manual or product teardown poster.
Background: clean white or light neutral tone.
Lighting: neutral studio lighting with soft shadows for depth.
Materials and textures must look realistic and detailed.
Use modern sans-serif typography with clear hierarchy (bold headings, smaller technical labels).
Ultra-detailed, sharp focus, 4K resolution, professional educational infographic style.
No artistic distortion, no clutter, no decorative elements unrelated to structure.
```

#### 📖 Description

A dual prompt used to transform an architectural pencil sketch of a wooden pergola into a photorealistic image of the fully built structure in a garden. The prompt ensures the final image matches the exact structure and proportions of the hand-drawn blueprint.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Pergola Blueprint Prompt: Hand-drawn architectural pencil sketch of a wooden garden pergola on white paper. Front and slight side elevation view with clear proportions.

Construction lines, dimensions and handwritten notes visible. Wooden posts and cross beams precisely drafted. Professional but hand-sketched technical style. Clean white background.

Finished Pergola: Fully built wooden pergola in a real garden, matching the exact structure and proportions from the sketch. Viewed from a low front corner perspective at eye level, showing depth and the full beam structure instead of a flat elevation. Natural wood texture with realistic joints and connections. Installed on grass with surrounding plants. Soft daylight with grounded shadows. Photorealistic architectural visualization.
```

#### 📖 Description

A prompt for generating a conceptual architectural isometric model (diorama style) of an organic-form corporate office tower, viewed from a top-down perspective, emphasizing fluid spatial flow, soft pastel colors, ribbed wood, and controlled studio lighting.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A conceptual architectural isometric model of an organic-form corporate office tower, presented as an exhibition-scale diorama. The building is shown with a cutaway section, exposing fluid spatial flow, curved meeting rooms, and lounge zoning from a top-down isometric perspective. Volumes are lightly separated by seamlessly curved translucent partitions to suggest circulation and function. Warm micro-LEDs illuminate the individual floors from within. Strong use of negative space and clear compositional hierarchy. Thin structural outlines, soft pastel lavender and mint, ribbed wood and dark terrazzo tones, minimal abstract signage, subtle human-scale markers. Smooth surfaces, pill-shaped rounded edges, dramatic controlled studio lighting, architectural model photography style, clean dark neutral background, high-detail yet minimal.
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly detailed, structured prompt for Gemini Nano Banana Pro, requesting a hyperrealistic image of the Turkish dish Köfte & Patates rendered as a technical architectural blueprint. It specifies colors, lighting, labeling (CAD style), ingredients, and mood, aiming for clinical precision and appetite appeal.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Technical architectural blueprint of KÖFTE & PATATES on deep navy blue drafting paper #0A1628. Hyperrealistic photorealistic food hero centered and dramatically lit — warm studio spotlight from upper left, soft fill from right, visible steam wisps and sauce drips frozen mid-motion in full color. Food rendered with obsessive detail: glistening textures, visible moisture, char marks, melting elements catching light. Blueprint linework in crisp electric blue #4A90D9 — precise measurement lines, dimension arrows, cross-section callouts, grid overlay, compass rose bottom-left, scale bar bottom-center, revision block top-right.
LABELS: Each ingredient connected by a thin glowing electric blue line with a sharp 90-degree elbow joint — exactly like a professional CAD drawing. Label text in bright white Helvetica Neue Light uppercase, ingredient name bold, descriptor below in thin italic: "INGREDIENT NAME — qty: X — preparation method, texture descriptor, origin".
Food positioned left-of-center. All labels clustered right side and top, lines fanning outward cleanly with no crossovers.
Main components from top to bottom:
- PİDE EKMEĞİ — butter-toasted, golden edges, 110mm DIA. sesame-sprinkled optional.
- DOMATES DİLİMLERİ — vine-ripened, jewel-red, thick-cut 8mm THICK.
- SOĞAN HALKALARI — thinly sliced, sharp, translucent.
- KÖFTE — qty: 3-4 — 80g each, hand-minced dana kıyma + baharat (kimyon, pul biber, soğan rendesi, maydanoz), grilled/charred edges, juicy center, 90mm DIA smashed style.
- PATATES — qty: generous portion — ince uzun kesilmiş, double-fried, extra crispy exterior, fluffy interior, golden-brown, 120mm length avg.
- ZEYTİNYAĞLI SALÇA SOS / ACUKA — tangy-spicy, rich red, drizzled 25g.
- MAYDANOZ & SUMAK — fresh green, bright red dusting.
Bottom-right title block with double border: "DISH: KÖFTE & PATATES TABAK // CLASSIFICATION: TÜRK EGE USULÜ // BUILD SPEC: çıtır çıtır patates, sulu köfte, tam kararında pişmiş, bağımlılık garantili."
Mood: NASA food engineering meets Michelin lokanta — clinical precision with irresistible appetite appeal. Dramatic, extraordinary, craveable. 4K, tack sharp, electric blue linework glowing softly, warm food tones popping against cool navy.
```

#### 📖 Description

A prompt for generating an ultra-realistic cinematic movie scene featuring a young man (with identity locked to a reference photo) in a dynamic action pose, with a Venom-like symbiote bursting from his back. The prompt specifies the setting (dark industrial warehouse), lighting (sharp light beams, warm highlights), and camera angle (low and tilted) for a Hollywood action-poster vibe.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
16K ultra-realistic cinematic movie scene of the same young man (keep his real facial features and proportions exactly as in the reference photo, no changes to his face). He is in a dynamic action pose, slightly crouched forward as if ready to leap or attack, one knee bent, one hand pressing firmly on the ground, the other reaching out with intense motion. From his back, a Venom-like symbiote bursts outward, forming huge menacing tendrils and a snarling monstrous face rising above his shoulder. The dark industrial warehouse setting is filled with mist and drifting smoke, with sharp light beams slicing through the fog. Camera angle is low and slightly tilted for dramatic tension and high-energy impact. Add warm highlights, motion blur, lens flares, and subtle film-grain for a Hollywood action-poster vibe. Generate the face using the uploaded reference image with accurate likeness.
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly detailed, multi-section prompt written entirely in Japanese for generating an image in the style of a TV variety show, focusing on a funny cat mishap. The prompt specifies text content, color schemes (using hex codes), fonts (variety show style, friendly rounded text), layout, subject (cat failing a jump, making a funny face), and overall style (comedy, decisive moment, smartphone photo look).

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Please output 4 times using the following prompt:

Text
Main: {argument name="main text" default="Miracle at Broadcast Accident Level"}
Sub: {argument name="sub text" default="Funny Cat Mishap Collection"} (bottom banner)
Other: Why did that happen w (speech bubble), Decisive Moment (top right stamp)

Color Scheme
Background: General living room (with signs of life, no specific brand furniture).
Text: Pop colors like #FFA500 (orange) and #FFFF00 (yellow).
Accent: Colors for comic-style concentration lines.

Font
Main: Fun, extra-bold font typical of variety shows.
Sub: Friendly, rounded text.

Layout
Text Placement:
Large main title at the top. Add movement to the text (italics, curve).
Place 'w' or speech bubbles near the cat's face.
Decoration/Figures:
Main subject is a cat (or dog).
Moments like failing a jump and being in a strange posture, funny faces, or flipping things over.
Motion blur to emphasize movement (subject blur) and comic sound effect effects (Don!, Biyooon, etc.).
Show the messy room without hiding it.

Style
Pet video, mishap, comedy, decisive moment, sense of dynamism, friendly, smartphone photo style
```

A cinematic video prompt, generated by GPT-5 and used for Kling 3.0 Omni, describing a four-cut sequence of a cute woman painting a wall. The prompt specifies camera shots (extreme close-up, medium, artistic cut, wide shot), soft pastel tones, and shallow depth of field to create a warm atmosphere.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A cinematic video composed of four cuts, themed around a cute woman painting a wall. Smoothly transitioning to an extreme close-up of scooping paint onto a brush, a medium shot of her happily painting the wall, an artistic cut capturing dripping paint, and a wide shot of her smiling contentedly in front of the finished wall. The overall look uses soft pastel tones and shallow depth of field to create a unified, warm atmosphere.
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly structured prompt for generating a 3-frame vertical filmstrip showing a woman leaning out of a car window during a night drive. It requires preserving the identity from a reference image and focuses on cinematic night lighting (warm tungsten streetlights, bokeh headlights), subtle film grain, and micro facial expression changes across the three frames (dreamy, peaceful, melancholic).

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"generation_request": {
"meta_data": {
"tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
"task_type": "night_drive_filmstrip_3frame_micro_expression",
"language": "en",
"priority": "highest",
"version": "v1.0_NIGHT_CAR_WINDOW_FILMSTRIP_3FRAME_WARM_BOKEH"
},
"input": {
"mode": "image_to_image",
"reference_image_usage": "maximum",
"preserve_identity": true,
"preserve_facial_features": true,
"preserve_hairstyle": true,
"notes": "Use the reference as the primary anchor. Create a 3-frame horizontal film strip (three stacked widescreen frames). Same adult woman identity, same car window composition, same night street background. Only micro facial expression changes per frame. Warm tungsten streetlights, soft film grain, bokeh headlights, subtle lens flare. No text, no logos, no watermark."
},
"output_settings": {
"aspect_ratio": "9:16",
"orientation": "portrait",
"resolution_target": "ultra_high_res",
"num_images": 1,
"layout": {
"type": "filmstrip",
"frames": 3,
"direction": "vertical_stack",
"gutter": "thin_black",
"outer_border": "none",
"panel_consistency": "maximum"
},
"sharpness": "soft_film",
"grain": "true_film",
"render_style": "cinematic_night_film"
},
"scene": {
"concept": "night drive cinematic filmstrip",
"environment": "moving car at night, city street, distant headlights, streetlamp bokeh",
"lighting": {
"style": "warm night street lighting",
"key_light": "tungsten streetlights + soft bounce from car body",
"highlights": "gentle specular highlights on skin and car paint",
"avoid": "flash, daylight, HDR"
},
"camera": {
"lens": "50mm cinematic",
"look": "shallow depth of field, creamy bokeh, subtle anamorphic flare",
"focus": "eyes and face sharp enough, background very soft"
}
},
"subject": {
"type": "adult woman",
"pose": "leaning out of the car window with forearms resting on the window frame, head resting on arms",
"hair": "long dark hair loose, softly moving",
"makeup": "natural warm makeup, soft lips",
"anatomy_rules": "correct facial proportions, realistic arms and hands, no warped skin"
},
"frame_directions": {
"frame_01": "soft direct eye contact, calm dreamy expression",
"frame_02": "eyes gently closed or half-closed, peaceful sleepy moment",
"frame_03": "eyes open again, slightly more melancholic gaze, subtle micro-smile fade"
},
"style": {
"color_palette": "warm amber streetlights + deep teal night shadows",
"contrast": "low-medium",
"finish": "cinematic film scan: soft halation, grain, mild vignette"
},
"quality_control": {
"identity_lock": "strict",
"composition_lock": "maximum",
"avoid": [
"changin
```

#### 📖 Description

A detailed prompt for generating a dynamic video sequence of a subject inserting a glowing Ridewatch into a Ziku-Driver, triggering a 'mechanical reveal' transformation into armored form, concluding with a combat stance. Designed for video generation tools like Veo 3.1 or Nano Banana 2.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Subject inserts a glowing Ridewatch into the Ziku-Driver and spins it 360 degrees. A 'mechanical reveal' begins as white and gold armor plates materialize piece-by-piece, snapping onto the torso, shoulders, and legs with robotic precision. Rapidly rotating golden clock hands and glowing orange pixelated data streams wrap around her body against a giant glowing crescent moon background. The sequence concludes with the helmet visor flashing pink katakana eyes as she shifts into a powerful combat stance, executing a sharp martial arts strike with her white cape flowing majestically.
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly specific, cinematic prompt comparing Nano Banana Pro and Nano Banana 2. It describes a low-angle, wide shot of a 2027 astronaut from SpaceX walking down a desolate 1852 street, featuring a Tesla with rocket boosters, a burning jail, and a woman lighting dynamite, all shot on 35mm film with a muted color palette.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
low angle wide shot at 45-degree side view of an astronaut walking down a desolate street at night, the time period is 1852, the astronaut is from 2027 and works for space x. The street is dirt and the air is hazy with light fog, there are two horses by a water trough in front of a hotel on the left of frame, there is a sign on the hotel that says Hilton, and on the right in the background a small building with a sign that says jail is just beginning to burn on fire, flames from inside the windows and licking up out of the roof, none of the other buildings in the small town street are on fire. on the right in the foreground slightly out of focus is a tesla with rocket boosters strapped to the back of it and standing near it is a beautiful woman in a mid 1800's dress is lighting a stick of dynamite, she has a devious smirk on her face. this scene is shot on a 35mm film camera with kodak film that has a daylight hue and has been graded with a muted color pallet
```

#### 📖 Description

A story prompt designed to instruct an AI (Nano Banana 2 in Firefly) to read the opening of a specified novel and visually retell the beginning scene across five manga panels (one large, four small), using a classic black and white manga style with expressive ink lines.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Read the opening of the novel [{argument name="book" default="BOOK"}]  by  [{argument name="author" default="AUTHOR"}]. Create a manga page with one opening panel on the left side and four panels on the right side that visually retell the very beginning of the story. Each panel should follow the narrative progression of the opening scene. Classic black and white manga style, expressive ink lines, cinematic framing, strong storytelling composition.
```

#### 📖 Description

A detailed prompt for generating a photorealistic cinematic film still (no CGI) in the style of Mad Max: Fury Road. It emphasizes preserving the exact identity and imperfections of a subject from a reference photo, featuring them as a wasteland survivor gripping an improvised shotgun in a harsh, sun-blasted desert environment with strong teal and orange color grading.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Photorealistic cinematic film still live-action (no CGI). Preserve the subject's identity exactly from the reference photo: same facial structure, asymmetry, skin texture, pores, dirt, sweat. No beautification. Three-quarter body portrait, vertical. Torso angled sideways, head toward camera. Hands gripping a rugged improvised shotgun at waist level. Wasteland survivor - Mad Max: Fury Road visual language. Practical, battle-worn realism. Environment: open desert wasteland, orange sand and dust clouds, saturated turquoise sky. Background: slightly blurred post-apocalyptic pursuit vehicles, sense of imminent chase. Subtle motion blur and heat haze. Lighting: harsh overhead desert sun, extremely hard shadows. Rich detail in sun-bleached highlights and deep crushed shadows. Wardrobe & details: distressed leather, scarf around neck, dusty welding goggles on forehead. Windblown messy hair. Face smeared with engine grease and sand (naturalistic). Look & color: shot on 35mm film, Kodak Vision3 feel. Organic film grain, high contrast. Strong teal & orange grading, realistic skin tones. Emphasis on tactile realism: rusted metal, sand particles, sweat, grease, fabric weave. Final: raw, tense, sun-blasted, high-energy cinematic still.
```

#### 📖 Description

A detailed prompt designed to generate a hand-drawn treasure map on aged parchment, where the map elements transition into a three-dimensional adventure scene, complete with landmarks, dangers, and a guardian element.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A hand-drawn treasure map on aged parchment, with the adventure erupting into three-dimensional quest. {argument name="starting point" default="STARTING_POINT"} rises at the map's edge with {argument name="departure scene" default="DEPARTURE_SCENE"}, the dotted path becomes an actual trail traversing {argument name="terrain 1" default="TERRAIN_1"} with {argument name="danger 1" default="DANGER_1"}, passing {LANDMARK_1} where {OBSTACLE_1} awaits. {TERRAIN_2} features {WILDLIFE_1} and {ENVIRONMENTAL_HAZARD}. The X marking the spot glows beneath {FINAL_LOCATION}, treasure partially visible, {GUARDIAN_ELEMENT} protecting it. Miniature expedition party frozen mid-journey at various points along the route. Torn edges of the map become cliffs. Coffee stains become lakes. Burn marks become scorched earth from battles past. Candlelight flicker, adventure novel lighting, 8K, the map as the story it promises.
```

A prompt for generating an anime-style image using a specific 2-point perspective and keystoning camera angle, depicting a tense stealth scene split compositionally between a hiding woman and a patrolling male guard.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Anime character in a background, 2-point perspective with a keystoning angle, composition split in half: on the right, a 20-year-old woman is pressed against a wall, waiting and hiding; on the left, seen through a door frame, a male guard holding a submachine gun is walking stealthily.

Japanese style animation, full color, modern Madhouse style (2020s), detailed cel shading, cinematic post-processing, masterpiece.
```

#### 📖 Description

A prompt used to test Nano Banana 2's ability to color comics, requiring the model to translate Japanese text to Chinese, color the comic in a style matching the content and atmosphere, use master-level coloring, and maintain the original aspect ratio and perfect consistency.

#### 📖 Description

A complex instructional prompt template for generating a premium, crystal-clear acrylic box containing a mirrored kaleidoscope chamber. The scene depicts a famous Asian dish being prepared by its inventor, with the entire diorama repeating in infinite symmetrical patterns, fusing Michelin-star refinement with prismatic sculpture.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Do this for a famous Asian dish<instruction> Input A is a famous dish (photo or dish name). Deeply analyze its origin country or region, the historical or legendary figure most associated with its creation or popularization, traditional preparation methods, cultural significance, and signature sensory elements. Optional Input B is a style reference. If missing, fuse Michelin-star refinement with kaleidoscopic prism sculpture.
Goal: Premium crystal-clear acrylic box containing a mirrored kaleidoscope chamber where the dish scene repeats in infinite symmetrical patterns.
Rules:
Center: single inventor figurine preparing the dish at the focal point, reflected into a mesmerizing symmetrical diorama
Ingredient compartments arranged in geometric radial symmetry
Miniature tools duplicated in the mirrored reflections
Origin map fractured and repeated as a mandala backdrop
Box edges create rainbow refractions from flag-inspired tints
Bilingual label positioned so it multiplies beautifully in the reflections with origin date
Output: ONE image, 4:5 artisan food photography style
</instruction>
```

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"master_prompt": {
"scene_type": "high-speed commercial luxury burger photography",
"product": {
"type": "premium gourmet burger with glossy brioche bun",
"brand_name": "ROYAL STACK",
"label_design": "subtle embossed gold foil logo on a matte black burger wrap",
"main_components": "double flame-grilled beef patties, aged cheddar, fresh lettuce, heirloom tomato slices, caramelized onions, signature sauce"
},
"composition": {
"action": "dramatic high-velocity ingredient explosion captured mid-assembly",
"surrounding_elements": [
"flame-kissed beef patties descending into place",
"melted cheese cascading in silky folds",
"fresh crisp lettuce leaves suspended mid-air",
"juicy tomato slices with visible seeds and texture",
"glossy sauce splashes forming sculptural arcs",
"sesame seeds scattering dynamically"
],
"placement": "centered hero burger with a sharp reflection on a polished dark stone surface"
},
"lighting": {
"style": "luxury cinematic studio food lighting",
"effects": [
"dramatic rim lighting to define bun and patty texture",
"brilliant highlights on melted cheese and sauce gloss",
"warm directional key light enhancing grill texture",
"soft smoky backlight for depth and richness"
]
},
"color_palette": {
"background": "deep charcoal fading into warm amber glow",
"accents": "golden brioche, rich brown beef, vibrant green lettuce, deep red tomato, molten cheddar tones"
},
"technical_specs": {
"camera": "macro lens, slight low angle for dominance",
"shutter": "ultra-fast freeze-motion capture",
"depth_of_field": "shallow focus on the front bun logo and cheese melt, gentle blur on flying ingredients",
"quality": "8K resolution, ultra-photorealistic texture"
}
}
}
```

#### 📖 Description

A detailed prompt for Nano Banana 2 to generate a cinematic, highly detailed portrait of Tom Cruise with a gritty, industrial aesthetic. The prompt specifies facial features, clothing (distressed leather jacket, stained apron), props (cigarette, signet ring), setting (industrial laboratory/soap factory), lighting (high-contrast neon), and includes embedded text branding ('GUCCI', 'IS AI STILL JUST A PLAYTHING?').

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A highly detailed, cinematic portrait of {argument name="subject name" default="Tom Cruise"}, featuring a gritty, industrial aesthetic. He has messy, spiked dark-blonde hair, facial stubble, and a look of intense, weary focus. He is captured in a close-up shot, holding a lit cigarette to his mouth with his right hand; a thick trail of white smoke curls upward. On his finger is a prominent silver signet ring engraved with a luxury interlocking "GG" logo. The man wears a distressed, dark red leather jacket heavily marked with white paint splatters and deep creases, layered over a filthy, stained cream-colored canvas apron. The center of the frame features the word "GUCCI" in a clean, white, authoritative serif font. Below it, in smaller text on the apron, is the phrase "IS AI STILL JUST A PLAYTHING?" followed by a second, faded "GUCCI" logo. The background is a blurred industrial laboratory or "soap factory" setting. To the right are stacks of translucent pink rectangular soap blocks. In the upper background, dark industrial machinery and hanging wires are illuminated by glowing red horizontal neon lights, creating a moody, high-contrast atmosphere. The entire image has a heavy film grain, a hazy atmosphere, and a color palette dominated by deep reds, pale pinks, and grimy earth tones.
```

#### 📖 Description

A photorealistic studio portrait prompt focusing on a young woman wearing a spider-themed bodysuit, emphasizing soft diffused lighting, high detail, and specific camera settings (85mm lens, shallow depth of field). Includes a negative prompt for quality control.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"prompt": "Photorealistic studio portrait of a young woman with long wavy red hair and fair skin, sitting on a concrete floor against a neutral gray backdrop. She is wearing a form-fitting red and blue spider-themed bodysuit with web patterns, slightly off-shoulder styling. The pose is relaxed and confident, leaning back on one hand with soft natural expression and subtle smile. Soft diffused studio lighting, cinematic tones, high detail, sharp focus, 85mm lens, shallow depth of field, natural skin texture, professional editorial photography.",
"negative_prompt": "blurry, low resolution, distorted anatomy, extra fingers, bad hands, harsh lighting, overexposed highlights, underexposed shadows, watermark, text, logo",
"style": "photorealistic",
"lighting": "soft diffused studio lighting",
"camera": "85mm lens, shallow depth of field",
"resolution": "1024x1536"
}
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly structured prompt for generating an ultra-photorealistic, high-speed commercial photograph of a luxury mango shake bottle, focusing on a dynamic splash explosion, specific lighting effects (rim lighting, golden glow), and technical camera details (macro lens, freeze-motion capture).

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"master_prompt": {
"scene_type": "high-speed commercial luxury shake photography",
"product": {
"type": "elegant frosted glass bottle filled with velvety mango shake",
"brand_name": "{argument name="Brand Name" default="MANGO AURÉLIA"}",
"label_design": "minimalist vertical ivory label with embossed brushed-gold serif typography",
"liquid_color": "creamy golden mango with rich natural pulp swirls"
},
"composition": {
"action": "dynamic high-velocity creamy mango splash explosion",
"surrounding_elements": [
"sculptural waves of thick mango shake splashing outward",
"silky ribbons of rich mango puree suspended mid-air",
"floating fresh mango cubes with juicy glossy texture",
"mango slices with vibrant yellow-orange flesh",
"fine droplets of creamy golden mist catching the light"
],
"placement": "centered hero bottle with a crisp reflection on a polished ivory marble surface"
},
"lighting": {
"style": "luxury glossy studio product lighting",
"effects": [
"sharp rim lighting to define bottle silhouette",
"brilliant highlights on creamy splashes and glossy mango surfaces",
"soft warm golden glow in the background for richness and elegance"
]
},
"color_palette": {
"background": "smooth gradient of warm mango gold fading into soft champagne cream",
"accents": "vibrant mango yellow, tropical orange, brushed gold, and vanilla cream tones"
},
"technical_specs": {
"camera": "macro lens, eye-level angle",
"shutter": "ultra-fast freeze-motion capture",
"depth_of_field": "shallow focus on the label, gentle blur on dynamic splash elements",
"quality": "8K resolution, ultra-photorealistic texture"
}
}
}
```

Overall aesthetic: luxurious, fresh, energetic, high-end commercial beverage advertising.",

```
"resolution": "Ultra-HD",

}
}

```
```
Ultra-realistic luxury beauty product photography featuring a transparent amber-toned body oil bottle labeled “{argument name="product name" default="the act"}” with the variant name “{argument name="variant name" default="VIRGIN"}” clearly visible on the front. Fine condensation droplets cover the glass
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly structured prompt for generating a high-speed commercial photograph of a luxury strawberry shake bottle ('ROSÉ VELVET'). It details the product, composition (dynamic creamy splash explosion, floating strawberries), lighting (luxury glossy studio lighting, rim lighting), color palette, and technical specs (macro lens, ultra-fast freeze-motion capture).

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"master_prompt": {
"scene_type": "high-speed commercial luxury shake photography",
"product": {
"type": "elegant frosted glass bottle filled with velvety strawberry shake",
"brand_name": "ROSÉ VELVET",
"label_design": "minimalist vertical blush label with embossed rose-gold serif typography",
"liquid_color": "creamy pastel pink with natural strawberry swirls"
},
"composition": {
"action": "dynamic high-velocity creamy splash explosion",
"surrounding_elements": [
"sculptural waves of thick strawberry shake splashing outward",
"silky ribbons of strawberry puree suspended mid-air",
"floating fresh strawberry halves with visible seeds and juicy texture",
"soft dusting of powdered sugar diffusing delicately",
"fine droplets of creamy pink mist catching the light"
],
"placement": "centered hero bottle with a crisp reflection on a polished white marble surface"
},
"lighting": {
"style": "luxury glossy studio product lighting",
"effects": [
"sharp rim lighting to define bottle silhouette",
"brilliant highlights on creamy splashes and glossy strawberry surfaces",
"soft rosy glow in the background for warmth and elegance"
]
},
"color_palette": {
"background": "smooth gradient of blush pink fading into soft champagne ivory",
"accents": "fresh strawberry red, rose gold, and creamy vanilla tones"
},
"technical_specs": {
"camera": "macro lens, eye-level angle",
"shutter": "ultra-fast freeze-motion capture",
"depth_of_field": "shallow focus on the label, gentle blur on dynamic splash elements",
"quality": "8K resolution, ultra-photorealistic texture"
}
}
}
```

#### 📖 Description

A highly specific, photorealistic prompt for a commercial product photograph of a Dior perfume bottle. The key feature is a single, continuous horizontal tear in the background paper that visually cuts through the glass bottle, revealing a dense, vibrant layer of citrus zest inside the tear channel.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
"A photorealistic, highly detailed commercial product photograph of a heavy cylindrical midnight-blue glass Dior perfume bottle centered against a minimal, textured dusky twilight blue paper background, with subtle fibers visible. A single, continuous horizontal paper-tear strip runs across the entire width of the frame, seamless from the far-right edge to the far-left edge. The torn channel cuts through the thick glass body of the referance perfume bottle and continues across the background on both sides with a barely perceptible and minimal diagonal slope towards the upper-left. The tear channel is significantly broader, revealing a dense, glistening, and vibrant layer of citrus and bright bergamot zest tightly packed inside. Near the extreme far-left end of this tear, the paper is neatly rolled into a tight, realistic coil that occupies almost the entire left margin. The lighting is soft and cinematic, casting delicate drop shadows beneath the torn paper edges and the large left coil. 8k resolution, macro details on fibrous paper and glossy citrus texture."
```

#### 📖 Description

A dual prompt set for transforming an uploaded product image into a hyper-realistic edible dessert. The first prompt (Nano Banana 2) focuses on generating a static image with a soft matte mousse texture and natural daylight. The second prompt (Veo 3.1) focuses on generating a slow-motion video where a spoon gently cuts the edible product, revealing layered cake inside.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Nano Banana 2 :- Hyper realistic edible version of uploaded product,preserve exact original shape, proportions and silhouette, product fully intact and uncut, surface transformed into soft matte mousse texture, natural sponge-like edible detailing, no glossy or shiny finish, subtle velvet spray coating, straight front view, eye-level camera angle, camera positioned directly in front of the product, centered symmetrical composition, natural soft daylight coming from a nearby window, realistic gentle shadows, placed on a wooden or beige marble table, soft neutral linen fabric background, bright natural lighting, not overexposed, shallow depth of field, macro lens photography, premium yet natural dessert styling, hand holding a simple spoon close to the surface but not cutting, ultra realistic, 8k high detailfinal image in 3:4.

Veo 3.1 :- Turn the uploaded product into a hyper realistic edible cake, preserve exact shape and front view. Soft matte mousse texture, no gloss.Natural daylight setting.A spoon gently cuts one edge, surface breaks softly, inside reveals layered sponge cake and cream.Slow smooth motion, macro lens, ultra realistic.
```

```
A pastel pink vintage Volkswagen Beetle driving along a scenic mountain highway, massive olive green luxury handbag strapped to the car roof with thick rope, surreal oversized fashion accessory, alpine landscape with tall rocky mountains and pine forests, soft golden hour lighting, cinematic travel photography, motion blur on road, ultra-detailed, realistic shadows, shallow depth of field, high resolution, editorial fashion campaign style, whimsical luxury concept

Prompt 2:

```

#### 📖 Description

A structured JSON prompt for generating a photorealistic 8k Octane render of a product (placeholder) centered and levitating in a pitch-black void. The prompt specifies technical details like rim lighting, high-contrast chiaroscuro, specific metallic textures (titanium, aluminum), and optical effects like fluted glass distortion.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
"image_generation_request": {
"subject": {
"placeholder": "{argument name="product name" default="INSERT PRODUCT HERE"}",
"position": "dead center, eye-level, perfectly centered in frame",
"presentation": "levitating, floating in a weightless void, no stands, no platforms, no pedestals, no visible supports"
},
"environment": {
"background": "absolute solid pitch black, hex code #000000, infinite void",
"lighting": "rim lighting, cinematic chiaroscuro, high-contrast glints on metallic edges, soft orange glow from internal recesses"
},
"aesthetic_style": {
"textures": [
"shot-blasted matte titanium",
"brushed aerospace-grade aluminum",
"anodized dark grey metal",
"ribbed industrial vertical cooling fins"
],
"optical_effects": "viewed through vertical fluted reeded glass, subtle chromatic distortion, refractive glass warping, radial motion blur on outer edges",
"color_palette": {
"primary_background": "#000000",
"secondary_metal": "#8E8E8E",
"accent_glow": "#FF8C00",
"highlights": "#FFFFFF"
}
},
"composition_constraints": {
"framing": "ensure full product visibility, do not cut off edges, wide safety margins around the subject",
"elements_to_exclude": [
"text",
"logos",
"watermarks",
"softboxes",
"light stands",
"floor",
"horizon line",
"props",
"human hands",
"shadows on surfaces"
]
},
"technical_specs": {
"focal_length": "85mm macro lens",
"depth_of_field": "deep focus on product, blurred refractive foreground glass",
"render_engine": "photorealistic octane render, 8k resolution, ray-traced reflections"
}
}
}
```

#### 📖 Description

A detailed prompt for generating ultra-realistic luxury cosmetic product photography. It focuses on a periwinkle-blue squeeze tube surrounded by swirling, ink-like clouds of blue and violet smoke, interwoven with fresh purple and lilac flowers, emphasizing dramatic monochromatic composition and high-end advertising style.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Ultra-realistic luxury cosmetic product photography in a dramatic monochromatic blue-violet composition. A sleek satin-finish periwinkle-blue squeeze tube labeled “{argument name="brand name" default="STENDERS"}” stands vertically centered in the frame, featuring elegant silver typography and a reflective metallic chrome cap. The tube is surrounded by voluminous swirling clouds of ink-like smoke in layered shades of lavender, indigo, and icy blue, creating a soft, billowing texture that wraps around the product. Interwoven throughout the smoke are fresh purple and lilac flowers with intricate petal details and vibrant yellow centers, along with delicate sprigs of tiny violet blossoms for added dimension. Soft directional lighting from the upper left highlights the smooth curvature of the tube, adds subtle sheen to the metallic cap, and creates depth within the smoke plumes. The background seamlessly blends cool blue and purple tones, enhancing the ethereal, floral fragrance aesthetic. Hyper-detailed textures of petals and vapor, cinematic depth, high-end perfumed skincare advertising style, 8K ultra-high resolution
```
